--- CBR-CDB ---
how much does other gastrostomy cost?
----------
PREDS:
select distinct cost.cost from cost where cost.event_type = 'procedures_icd' and cost.event_id in ( select procedures_icd.row_id from procedures_icd where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'other gastrostomy' ) )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost'], 'tab': ['cost'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'other gastrostomy ) )']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select distinct cost.cost from cost where cost.event_type = 'procedures_icd' and cost.event_id in ( select procedures_icd.row_id from procedures_icd where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'other gastrostomy' ) )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost'], 'tab': ['cost'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'other gastrostomy ) )']]}
----------

what is gentamicin's price?
----------
PREDS:
select distinct cost.cost from cost where cost.event_type = 'prescriptions' and cost.event_id in ( select prescriptions.row_id from prescriptions where prescriptions.drug = 'gentamicin' )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost'], 'tab': ['cost'], 'cond': [['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'gentamicin )']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select distinct cost.cost from cost where cost.event_type = 'labevents' and cost.event_id in ( select labevents.row_id from labevents where labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'gentamicin' ) )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost'], 'tab': ['cost'], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'gentamicin ) )']]}
----------

what does echinococc granul nos mean?
----------
PREDS:
select d_icd_diagnoses.long_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'echinococcosis nec/nos' union select d_icd_procedures.long_title from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'echinococcosis nec/nos'
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'echinococcosis nec/nos']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select d_icd_diagnoses.long_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'echinococc granul nos' union select d_icd_procedures.long_title from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'echinococc granul nos'
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'echinococc granul nos']]}
----------

select gender from patients where subject_id = 23620

how much difference is patient 60136's glucose level second measured on the current hospital visit compared to the first value measured on the current hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
select ( select chartevents.valuenum from chartevents where chartevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 60136 and admissions.dischtime is null ) and chartevents.itemid in ( select d_items.itemid from d_items where d_items.label = 'glucose' and d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) order by chartevents.charttime asc limit 1 offset 1 ) - ( select chartevents.valuenum from chartevents where chartevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 60136 and admissions.dischtime is null ) and chartevents.itemid in ( select d_items.itemid from d_items where d_items.label = 'glucose' and d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) order by chartevents.charttime asc limit 1 )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['chartevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['chartevents'], 'cond': [['d_items.label', '=', 'glucose'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents ) order by chartevents.charttime asc limit 1 )']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select ( select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 60136 and admissions.dischtime is null ) and labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'glucose' ) order by labevents.charttime asc limit 1 offset 1 ) - ( select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 60136 and admissions.dischtime is null ) and labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'glucose' ) order by labevents.charttime asc limit 1 )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['labevents'], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'glucose ) order by labevents.charttime asc limit 1 )']]}
----------

how much does patient 2425's ptt change from the last value measured on the first hospital visit compared to the second to last value measured on the first hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
select (select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in (select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 2425 order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1) and labevents.itemid in (select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'ptt') order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1) - (select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in (select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 2425 order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1) and labevents.itemid in (select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'ptt') order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1)
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['labevents'], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'ptt ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1)']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select ( select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 2425 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1 ) and labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'ptt' ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 ) - ( select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 2425 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1 ) and labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'ptt' ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['labevents'], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'ptt ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )']]}

----------

is the ph level of patient 1918 last measured on the first hospital visit greater than second to last measured on the first hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
select ( select chartevents.valuenum from chartevents where chartevents.icustay_id in ( select icustays.icustay_id from icustays where icustays.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 1918 ) and icustays.outtime is not null order by icustays.intime asc limit 1 ) and chartevents.itemid in ( select d_items.itemid from d_items where d_items.label = 'ph level' and d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) order by chartevents.charttime desc limit 1 ) > ( select chartevents.valuenum from chartevents where chartevents.icustay_id in ( select icustays.icustay_id from icustays where icustays.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 1918 ) and icustays.outtime is not null order by icustays.intime asc limit 1 ) and chartevents.itemid in ( select d_items.itemid from d_items where d_items.label = 'ph level' and d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) order by chartevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['chartevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['chartevents'], 'cond': [['d_items.label', '=', 'ph level'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents ) order by chartevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select ( select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 1918 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1 ) and labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'ph' ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 ) > ( select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 1918 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1 ) and labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'ph' ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['labevents'], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'ph ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )']]}

how many days has elapsed since the last time that patient 18866 took a or crystalloid intake on the current intensive care unit visit?
----------
PREDS:
select 1 * ( strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',chartevents.charttime) ) from chartevents where chartevents.icustay_id in ( select icustays.icustay_id from icustays where icustays.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 18866 and admissions.dischtime is null ) ) and chartevents.itemid = (select itemid from d_items where label = 'crystalloid intake') order by chartevents.charttime desc limit 1
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['chartevents.charttime'], 'tab': ['chartevents'], 'cond': [['label', '=', 'crystalloid intake ) order by chartevents.charttime desc limit 1']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select 1 * ( strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',inputevents_cv.charttime) ) from inputevents_cv where inputevents_cv.icustay_id in ( select icustays.icustay_id from icustays where icustays.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 18866 ) and icustays.outtime is null ) and inputevents_cv.itemid in ( select d_items.itemid from d_items where d_items.label = 'or crystalloid' and d_items.linksto = 'inputevents_cv' ) order by inputevents_cv.charttime desc limit 1
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['inputevents_cv.charttime'], 'tab': ['inputevents_cv'], 'cond': [['d_items.label', '=', 'or crystalloid'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'inputevents_cv ) order by inputevents_cv.charttime desc limit 1']]}
----------

what is the first id of the ward of patient 630 in 2103?
----------
PREDS:
select transfers.wardid from transfers where transfers.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 630 ) and transfers.wardid is not null and datetime(transfers.intime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') order by transfers.intime asc limit 1
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['transfers.wardid'], 'tab': ['transfers'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '630 )'], ["datetime(transfers.intime,'start", 'of', 'year ) = datetime(current_time, start of year , -0 year ) order by transfers.intime asc limit 1'], ['transfers.wardid', 'is', 'not null']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select transfers.wardid from transfers where transfers.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 630 ) and transfers.wardid is not null and strftime('%y',transfers.intime) = '2103' order by transfers.intime asc limit 1
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['transfers.wardid'], 'tab': ['transfers'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '630 )'], ["strftime('%y',transfers.intime)", '=', '2103 order by transfers.intime asc limit 1'], ['transfers.wardid', 'is', 'not null']]}

----------

{'sel': '', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.short_title'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '72909 )'], ['diagnoses_icd.charttime', '>=', 'date( now , -1 year ) order by diagnoses_icd.charttime desc limit 1 )']]}
----------
TRUTH:

{'sel': '', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.short_title'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '72909 )'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', 'datetime(current_time, -1 year ) order by diagnoses_icd.charttime desc limit 1 )']]}

----------

retrieve the top five most frequent treatments for patients of age 40s?
----------
PREDS:
select t1.icd9_code from ( select procedures_icd.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from procedures_icd where procedures_icd.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.age between 40 and 49 ) group by procedures_icd.icd9_code ) as t1 where t1.c1 <= 5
{'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}

how many patients expired within 2 months after being diagnosed with late effect cv dis nos since 4 years ago?
PRED: select count( distinct t2.subject_id ) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'late effect cv dis nos' ) ) as t1 group by t1.subject_id having min(t1.charttime) = t1.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime) <= datetime(current_time,'-4 year') ) as t2 join ( select patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod from admissions join patients on patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id where patients.dod is not null and datetime(patients.dod) <= datetime(current_time,'-4 year') ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where ( datetime(t3.dod) between datetime(t2.charttime) and datetime(t2.charttime,'+2 month') ) or ( datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t3.dod) and datetime(t3.dod,'+2 month') )
GOLD: select count( distinct t2.subject_id ) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'late effect cv dis nos' ) ) as t1 group by t1.subject_id having min(t1.charttime) = t1.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-4 year') ) as t2 join ( select patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod from admissions join patients on patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id where patients.dod is not null and datetime(patients.dod) >= datetime(current_time,'-4 year') ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where ( datetime(t3.dod) between datetime(t2.charttime) and datetime(t2.charttime,'+2 month') ) or ( datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t3.dod) and datetime(t3.dod,'+2 month') )
PRED_OUT: Counter({(0,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(1,): 1})

tell me the total number of patients having a urine,kidney test since 2 years ago?
PRED: select count(distinct admissions.subject_id) from admissions where admissions.hadm_id in (select labevents.hadm_id from labevents where labevents.itemid in (select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label in ('urine', 'kidney')) and datetime(labevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-2 year'))
GOLD: select count( distinct admissions.subject_id ) from admissions where admissions.hadm_id in ( select microbiologyevents.hadm_id from microbiologyevents where microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'urine,kidney' and datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-2 year') )
PRED_OUT: Counter({(0,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(5,): 1})

until 2104, what are the top five most common diagnoses that patients were diagnosed with in the same month after receiving endovasc embol hd/nk ves?
PRED: select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t3.icd9_code from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'endovasc embol hd/nk ves' ) and datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) <= datetime(current_time,'start of year','+80 year') ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) <= datetime(current_time,'start of year','+80 year') ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 5 )
GOLD: select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t3.icd9_code from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'endovasc embol hd/nk ves' ) and strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) <= '2104' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) <= '2104' ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 5 )
PRED_OUT: Counter()
GOLD_OUT: Counter({('hypertension nos',): 1, ('atrial fibrillation',): 1, ('crbl emblsm w infrct',): 1, ('accident in place nec',): 1})

what are the five most commonly used procedures for patients that have previously received hypothermia within 2 months, since 2101?
PRED: select d_icd_procedures.short_title from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.icd9_code in ( select t3.icd9_code from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'hypothermia' ) and strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2101' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd9_code, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2101' ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t1.charttime) and datetime(t1.charttime,'+2 month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 5 )
GOLD: select d_icd_procedures.short_title from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.icd9_code in ( select t3.icd9_code from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'hypothermia' ) and strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2101' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd9_code, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2101' ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t1.charttime) and datetime(t1.charttime,'+2 month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 5 )
PRED_OUT: Counter({('inject/infus nesiritide',): 1, ('aortocor bypas-1 cor art',): 1, ('vasc proc revision nec',): 1, ('bronch/trach lavage nec',): 1, ('cont inv mec ven <96 hrs',): 1})

what is the four most frequent drug that was prescribed within 2 months to the female patients aged 40s after they have been diagnosed with acute respiratry failure in the last year?
PRED: select t3.drug from ( select t2.drug, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute respiratory failure' ) and datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where admissions.subject_id in ( select patients.subject_id from patients where patients.gender = 'f' ) and admissions.age between 40 and 49 and datetime(prescriptions.startdate) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate and datetime(t2.startdate) between datetime(t1.charttime) and datetime(t1.charttime,'+2 month') group by t2.drug ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 4
GOLD: select t3.drug from ( select t2.drug, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'acute respiratry failure' ) and datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where admissions.subject_id in ( select patients.subject_id from patients where patients.gender = 'f' ) and admissions.age between 40 and 49 and datetime(prescriptions.startdate,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate and datetime(t2.startdate) between datetime(t1.charttime) and datetime(t1.charttime,'+2 month') group by t2.drug ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 4
